            task.spawn(function()

                    game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage");
                    ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Events"):WaitForChild("Admin"):WaitForChild("Command"):InvokeServer(
                        "!specialround " .. p1_39
                    );
                end) then
                else 
                    print("[SpecialRound] Set to:", p1_39);
                end;
            end);
        end;
        Main_Lua:Notify({
            ["Icon"] = "circle-check",
            ["Duration"] = 2,
            ["Title"] = "Special Round",
            ["Content"] = "Setting special round: " .. p1_39
        });
    end,
    ["Icon"] = "zap",
    ["Desc"] = "Manually set special round now"
});
